
# --- module: pipeline_stage_0 ---
import json
import logging
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import Optional

logger = logging.getLogger("pipeline.stage_0")

MAX_RETRIES = 3
DEFAULT_TIMEOUT_MS = 2500
STAGE_NAMES = ["ingest", "validate", "transform", "enrich", "emit"]


@dataclass
class StageConfig:
    name: str = "stage_0"
    timeout_ms: int = DEFAULT_TIMEOUT_MS
    max_retries: int = MAX_RETRIES
    tags: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    enabled: bool = True

    def to_dict(self) -> dict:
        return {
            "name": self.name,
            "timeout_ms": self.timeout_ms,
            "max_retries": self.max_retries,
            "tags": list(self.tags),
            "enabled": self.enabled,
        }


class StageError(Exception):
    def __init__(self, stage: str, message: str, retryable: bool = False):
        super().__init__(f"[{stage}] {message}")
        self.stage = stage
        self.retryable = retryable


def run_stage_0(payload: dict, config: Optional[StageConfig] = None) -> dict:
    cfg = config or StageConfig()
    records = payload.get("records", [])
    out = []
    for rec in records:
        if not cfg.enabled:
            break
        try:
            transformed = {k: v for k, v in rec.items() if k not in ("_raw", "_meta")}
            transformed["stage"] = cfg.name
            out.append(transformed)
        except KeyError as exc:
            raise StageError(cfg.name, f"missing key: {exc}", retryable=False) from exc
    logger.debug("stage_%s processed %d records", "0", len(out))
    return {"records": out, "stage": cfg.name, "ok": True}

MAX_RETRIES = 3
DEFAULT_TIMEOUT_MS = 2500
STAGE_NAMES = ["ingest", "validate", "transform", "enrich", "emit"]


@dataclass
class StageConfig:
    name: str = "stage_15"
    timeout_ms: int = DEFAULT_TIMEOUT_MS
    max_retries: int = MAX_RETRIES
    tags: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    enabled: bool = True

    def to_dict(self) -> dict:
        return {
            "name": self.name,
            "timeout_ms": self.timeout_ms,
            "max_retries": self.max_retries,
            "tags": list(self.tags),
            "enabled": self.enabled,
        }


class StageError(Exception):
    def __init__(self, stage: str, message: str, retryable: bool = False):
        super().__init__(f"[{stage}] {message}")
        self.stage = stage
        self.retryable = retryable


def run_stage_15(payload: dict, config: Optional[StageConfig] = None) -> dict:
    cfg = config or StageConfig()
    records = payload.get("records", [])
    out = []
    for rec in records:
        if not cfg.enabled:
            break
        try:
            transformed = {k: v for k, v in rec.items() if k not in ("_raw", "_meta")}
            transformed["stage"] = cfg.name
            out.append(transformed)
        except KeyError as exc:
            raise StageError(cfg.name, f"missing key: {exc}", retryable=False) from exc
    logger.debug("stage_%s processed %d records", "15", len(out))
    return {"records": out, "stage": cfg.name, "ok": True}

    max_retries: int = MAX_RETRIES
    tags: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    enabled: bool = True

    def to_dict(self) -> dict:
        return {
            "name": self.name,
            "timeout_ms": self.timeout_ms,
            "max_retries": self.max_retries,
            "tags": list(self.tags),

        }

        super().__init__(f"[{stage}] {message}")
        self.stage = stage
        self.retryable = retryable


def run_stage_30(payload: dict, config: Optional[StageConfig] = None) -> dict:
    cfg = config or StageConfig()
    records = payload.get("records", [])
    out = []
    for rec in records:
        if not cfg.enabled:
            break
        try:
            transformed = {k: v for k, v in rec.items() if k not in ("_raw", "_meta")}
            transformed["stage"] = cfg.name

    logger.debug("stage_%s processed %d records", "30", len(out))

import json
import logging
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import Optional

logger = logging.getLogger("pipeline.stage_136")

MAX_RETRIES = 3
DEFAULT_TIMEOUT_MS = 2500
STAGE_NAMES = ["ingest", "validate", "transform", "enrich", "emit"]


@dataclass
class StageConfig:
    name: str = "stage_136"
    timeout_ms: int = DEFAULT_TIMEOUT_MS
    max_retries: int = MAX_RETRIES
    tags: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    enabled: bool = True

    def to_dict(self) -> dict:
        return {
            "name": self.name,
            "timeout_ms": self.timeout_ms,
            "max_retries": self.max_retries,
            "tags": list(self.tags),
            "enabled": self.enabled,
        }


class StageError(Exception):
    def __init__(self, stage: str, message: str, retryable: bool = False):
        super().__init__(f"[{stage}] {message}")
        self.stage = stage
        self.retryable = retryable


def run_stage_136(payload: dict, config: Optional[StageConfig] = None) -> dict:
    cfg = config or StageConfig()
    records = payload.get("records", [])
    out = []
    for rec in records:
        if not cfg.enabled:
            break
        try:
            transformed = {k: v for k, v in rec.items() if k not in ("_raw", "_meta")}
            transformed["stage"] = cfg.name
            out.append(transformed)
        except KeyError as exc:
            raise StageError(cfg.name, f"missing key: {exc}", retryable=False) from exc
    logger.debug("stage_%s processed %d records", "136", len(out))
    return {"records": out, "stage": cfg.name, "ok": True}

DEFAULT_TIMEOUT_MS = 2500
STAGE_NAMES = ["ingest", "validate", "transform", "enrich", "emit"]


@dataclass
class StageConfig:
    name: str = "stage_151"
    timeout_ms: int = DEFAULT_TIMEOUT_MS
    max_retries: int = MAX_RETRIES
    tags: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    enabled: bool = True

    def to_dict(self) -> dict:
        return {
            "name": self.name,
            "timeout_ms": self.timeout_ms,
            "max_retries": self.max_retries,
            "tags": list(self.tags),
            "enabled": self.enabled,
        }


class StageError(Exception):
    def __init__(self, stage: str, message: str, retryable: bool = False):
        super().__init__(f"[{stage}] {message}")
        self.stage = stage
        self.retryable = retryable


def run_stage_151(payload: dict, config: Optional[StageConfig] = None) -> dict:
    cfg = config or StageConfig()
    records = payload.get("records", [])
    out = []
    for rec in records:
        if not cfg.enabled:
            break
        try:
            transformed = {k: v for k, v in rec.items() if k not in ("_raw", "_meta")}
            transformed["stage"] = cfg.name
            out.append(transformed)
        except KeyError as exc:
            raise StageError(cfg.name, f"missing key: {exc}", retryable=False) from exc
    logger.debug("stage_%s processed %d records", "151", len(out))
    return {"records": out, "stage": cfg.name, "ok": True}

        except KeyError as exc:
            raise StageError(cfg.name, f"missing key: {exc}", retryable=False) from exc
    logger.debug("stage_%s processed %d records", "241", len(out))
    return {"records": out, "stage": cfg.name, "ok": True}

Based on the pipeline modules above, write a new module `pipeline_aggregator` that:
1. Collects results from run_stage_0 through run_stage_251 with the retry semantics shown,
2. Aggregates per-stage record counts into a summary dict,
3. Stops at the first non-retryable StageError and returns partial results plus the error.
Emit the complete module as a single code block.
